Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the informatics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 860. The moduli space has dimension at most 585 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to informatics structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 860/585 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The informatics operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 676. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 913 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on informatics structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 676. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 913. QED.
